On Thursday the 6th lunar day, on Wednesday the 9th, on Sunday or Monday the 11th, on Tuesday the 5th, on Friday the 8th, and on Saturday the 9th; these are declared the <term>Dagdha-Yogas</term> <note>literally "Burned Alignments"; times when the energy is considered scorched and unproductive</note> arising from the combination of lunar day and weekday. One should always avoid these for auspicious acts. <insert>27</insert>

### Now, the avoidable alignments arising from lunar days and stars.

One should avoid the 2nd <term>tithi</term> <note>lunar day</note> when it falls on the star <term>Anuradha</term>, the 5th on <term>Magha</term>, the 11th on <term>Rohini</term>, the 3rd on <term>Uttara-Phalguni</term>, the 9th on <term>Krittika</term>, and the 13th on <term>Swati</term> or <term>Chitra</term>. One should also avoid the 6th on <term>Rohini</term> and the 8th on <term>Purva-Bhadrapada</term>. <insert>28</insert>

### Now, the Burned-Alignments arising from stars and weekdays: Death’s Bells.

Sunday with <term>Magha</term> or <term>Vishakha</term>, Monday with <term>Anuradha</term> or <term>Mula</term>, Tuesday with <term>Krittika</term>, Wednesday with <term>Ardra</term>, Thursday with <term>Hasta</term>, Friday with <term>Rohini</term>, and Saturday with <term>Uttara-Phalguni</term> <note>The text likely refers to the "Yama-ghanta" or "Death's Bell" periods</note>—this alignment named <term>Yama-ghanta</term> is the destroyer of all works.

### Now, the Death-Alignment.

<term>Anuradha</term> on Sunday, <term>Uttara-Ashadha</term> on Monday, <term>Ashvini</term> on Wednesday, <term>Mrigashira</term> on Thursday, <term>Ashlesha</term> on Friday, <term>Hasta</term> on Saturday, and <term>Shatabhisha</term> on Tuesday—this is the <term>Mrityu-yoga</term> <note>Death-Alignment</note>, which destroys wealth.

### Now, another Burned-Alignment from stars and weekdays.

One should avoid <term>Bharani</term> on Sunday, <term>Chitra</term> on Monday, <term>Uttara-Phalguni</term> on Thursday, <term>Purva-Ashadha</term> on Tuesday, <term>Anuradha</term> on Wednesday, <term>Jyeshtha</term> on Friday, and <term>Revati</term> on Saturday.

### Now, the three types of Knot-Ends to be avoided in auspicious acts.

The three types of <term>Gandanta</term> <note>original: "tri-vidha-gandanta"; literally "knot-end," a spiritual or temporal danger zone occurring at the junction of two signs, stars, or lunar days</note>—arising from stars, lunar days, and zodiac signs—should be avoided. In the beginning or end of these, a duration of half a <term>ghatika</term> <note>about 12 minutes</note> or up to four <term>ghatikas</term> <note>about 96 minutes</note> should be shunned. He specifically defines them: at the junction of <term>Jyeshtha</term> and <term>Mula</term>, likewise <term>Revati</term> and <term>Ashvini</term>, and between <term>Ashlesha</term> and <term>Magha</term>, one should avoid four <term>nadis</term> <note>units of time</note>. Regarding lunar days: the junction of the 5th and 6th, the Full Moon and the 1st, and the 10th and 11th, constitutes two <term>ghatikas</term> of <term>Gandanta</term>. Likewise, at the transition between Cancer and Leo, Pisces and Aries, and Scorpio and Sagittarius, the junction of the <term>Lagna</term> <note>the rising sign or ascendant</note> for the duration of one <term>ghatika</term> is to be avoided. <insert>35</insert>

### Now, the Half-Periods.

The <term>Ardha-yamas</term> <note>literally "Half-Watches," specific segments of the day</note> to be avoided are the 4th, 7th, 2nd, 5th, 8th, 3rd, and 6th, counting sequentially from Sunday onwards. <insert>30</insert>

### Now, the Kulika-Alignments and others.

The portions of the day measured as <term>Kulika</term> occur in order of the weekdays. <note>The text provides specific numerical codes for calculating these inauspicious "thorns" (kantaka)</note>. These periods, along with those known as <term>Kala-vela</term> <note>Time-Bands</note> and <term>Yama-ghanta</term>, should be avoided for auspicious works, though they are generally considered only for the daytime, with one specific <term>muhurta</term> <note>a 48-minute period</note> to be avoided at night. <insert>39</insert>

### Burned Directions.

<note>original: "dagdha-disha"</note>
The direction of the Sun <note>East</note> is "burned" on the 10th and 14th lunar days; for the Moon, the 9th and 12th; for Mars, the 7th and the night; for Mercury, the 8th.

### Now, another Burned-Alignment from stars and weekdays.

On Thursday, the 6th and 12th; on Friday, the 4th and 9th; on Saturday, the 2nd and 11th—these are the "Burned Directions" to be avoided. Again, for the sake of clarity, here are the avoided directions starting from the Sun and others...

<meta>This page continues the catalog of astrological warnings. While the previous page listed "Burned" (Dagdha) alignments and "Death’s Bells" (Yama-ghanta), this page provides specific calculations for "Void" (Shunya) times—units of time, lunar days, stars, and zodiac signs that are considered powerless or inauspicious for starting new ventures.</meta>

Now are recorded <gloss>adhi</gloss> the counts of <term>muhurtas</term> <note>units of time approximately 48 minutes long; there are 30 in a full day</note> to be avoided. <gloss>pri</gloss> On **Sunday**, the 14th, 12th, 3rd, 8th, and 14th units are considered void. On **Monday**, the 1st, 3rd, 8th, 5th, 14th, 9th, 12th, and 12th units are noted. On **Tuesday**, the 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 6th, and 10th are remembered. On **Wednesday**, the 4th, 2nd, 14th, and 10th units are the avoided moments. On **Thursday**, the 8th, 2nd, 15th, 2nd, and 14th units. On **Friday**, the 5th, 6th, 10th, 12th, and 14th units. On **Saturday**, the 8th, 2nd, 11th, 10th, 10th, and 12th units should be abandoned. These forty-five <insert>45</insert> units are collectively avoided across the sun’s cycles.

### Now, the units to be avoided during the day's transitions.

In the morning, afternoon, and night, specific times must be avoided for the worship of deities. Between the **Vindhya** and **Himalaya** mountains, six units in the south-east are avoided. In the regions of **Anga** and **Matsya**, the throat and hands should be avoided <note>This likely refers to specific "limbs" of the astrological personified day</note>. The middle half-watch <note>a "yama" is a 3-hour watch</note> is avoided everywhere. <insert>48</insert>

### Now, the avoidance of the Equinox.

During the <term>Vishuva</term> <note>equinox; when day and night are equal</note>, the first and middle parts of the day are avoided. During other <term>Sankrantis</term> <note>when the sun moves into a new zodiac sign</note>, twelve <term>nadikas</term> <note>units of 24 minutes</note> before and after the transition must be avoided. <insert>49</insert>

### Now, special rules for lunar days.

If three <term>tithis</term> <note>lunar days</note> occur within a single solar day, or if one lunar day spans three solar dates, or if Saturn and the Sun meet on a specific day, that day is considered <term>Avama</term> <note>low or decreased</note>. One should not perform auspicious works on such days. If three weekdays pass during a single lunar day, it is a "Three-Day-Toucher," which is forbidden for marriages and other festive rites. <insert>50</insert>

### Now, the Void Lunar Days of the months.

These culminate in the <term>Durga</term> <note>the end of the fortnight</note>. 
In the month of **Chaitra** <note>March-April</note>, the 8th and 9th lunar days are void. 
In **Vaishakha** <note>April-May</note>, the 12th lunar day. 
In **Shravana** <note>July-August</note>, the 3rd. 
In **Bhadrapada** <note>August-September</note>, the 2nd and 3rd. <insert>52</insert> 
The 10th and 11th in **Ashvina** <note>September-October</note>. 
The 7th and 8th in **Margashirsha** <note>November-December</note>. 
The 4th and 5th in **Pausha** <note>December-January</note>. These are void in both fortnights. <insert>53</insert> 
In **Kartika** <note>October-November</note>, the 5th of the dark fortnight and the 14th of the bright fortnight. 
In **Ashadha** <note>June-July</note>, the 6th of the dark and 7th of the bright fortnight. <insert>54</insert> 
In **Phalguna** <note>February-March</note>, the 4th of the dark and 3rd of the bright fortnight. 
In **Jyeshtha** <note>May-June</note>, the 14th of the dark and 13th of the bright. 
In **Chaitra**, also the 13th. <insert>55</insert> 
In **Magha** <note>January-February</note>, the 5th of the dark and 6th of the bright are declared void.

### Now, the Void Stars of the months.

The wise state these stars are void: 
**Chaitra**: <term>Ashvini</term> and <term>Rohini</term>. 
**Vaishakha**: <term>Chitra</term> and <term>Swati</term>. 
**Jyeshtha**: <term>Mula</term> and <term>Purva-Ashadha</term>. 
**Ashadha**: <term>Dhanishta</term> and <term>Abhijit</term>. <insert>57</insert> 
**Shravana**: <term>Shravana</term>. 
**Bhadrapada**: <term>Purva-Bhadrapada</term> and <term>Ashvini</term>. 
**Ashvina**: <term>Revati</term> and <term>Shatabhisha</term>. 
**Kartika**: <term>Krittika</term> and <term>Magha</term>. 
**Margashirsha**: <term>Chitra</term> and <term>Vishakha</term>. 
**Pausha**: <term>Ardra</term>, <term>Ashvini</term>, and <term>Hasta</term>. 
**Phalguna**: <term>Bharani</term> and <term>Jyeshtha</term>. 
**Magha**: <term>Mula</term> and <term>Shravana</term>. <insert>58</insert>

### Now, the Void Zodiac Signs of the months.

Starting from **Chaitra**, these are the void signs in order: **Aquarius**, **Pisces**, **Taurus**, **Gemini**, **Aries**, **Virgo**, <insert>59</insert> **Scorpio**, **Libra**, **Sagittarius**, **Capricorn**, **Leo**, and **Cancer**.

### Now, the Void Ascendants of the lunar days.

Regarding the <term>Lagnas</term> <note>ascendant signs rising on the eastern horizon</note> that are void: 
On the 2nd lunar day, **Capricorn** and **Leo** are void. 
On the 5th, **Gemini** and **Virgo**. 
On the 7th, **Cancer** and **Sagittarius**. <insert>62</insert>

<meta>This page continues the technical classification of inauspicious and auspicious timings. While the previous page focused on specific "void" units of time (muhurtas), this page discusses how these "void" (shunya) states apply to entire zodiac signs and lunar days, specifically noting regional variations and exceptions. The eleventh <note>Aquarius</note>, ninth <note>Sagittarius</note>, Leo, and Cancer; Taurus and Pisces; and the thirteenth—these <term>Lagnas</term> <note>rising signs; the zodiac sign ascending on the eastern horizon</note> should be abandoned. Now, the rule for not avoiding "Void" signs and similar states: Those lunar days and rising signs that are considered "Month-Voids" <note>Shunya; times when the presiding energy of the month is absent</note> should be avoided in the **Middle Country** <note>Madhyadesha; the central heartland of ancient Northern India</note>, but elsewhere they may be considered somewhat auspicious. 94

### Now, the exceptions to the "Void" and other faults.

One should avoid the faults arising from the <term>Tithi</term> <note>lunar day</note> and the <term>Nakshatra</term> <note>lunar mansion or star constellation</note> for those born under the influence of the sun or moon. These faults are avoided in Aries. 95 These are not forbidden in other regions, such as **Magadha** <note>modern-day Bihar</note> or among the **Bhatis**. One must carefully weigh the faults of portents, death-conjunctions, and the "Ghataka" <note>destructive</note> times. 96

The expert who understands the nature of the stars and the "Death’s Bell" <note>Yama-ghanta</note> should observe the moon. Some say these faults apply at midday or midnight, while others say they apply a <term>yama</term> <note>a three-hour watch</note> later. 8 In all deeds except for travel, a pure rising sign is auspicious. Even if there is a bad alignment, if a **Siddhi-yoga** <note>a "Union of Success"; a highly auspicious alignment that overcomes minor faults</note> occurs, then both are balanced. 98 <margin>Success</margin> That conjunction brings success in work and is auspicious, as the wise know.

In the section regarding **Vishkumbha** and other alignments, the **Bhadra** <note>an inauspicious period</note> and other unions depend on the day of the week. In the section on "even" or "uneven" days, the unions are known as a pair. 90 There, through the root of the stars, whether auspicious or inauspicious, the results are known respectively as being three-fold or two-fold in strength. 91

### Now, the Auspicious and Inauspicious Unions for the Days of the Week.

On **Sunday** (Ravi), the stars Hasta, Ashvini, Mula, Dhanishtha, and the three "Uttaras" <note>Uttara Phalguni, Uttara Ashadha, and Uttara Bhadrapada</note>, along with Pushya and the 8th lunar day, are known as **Siddhi-yogas** <note>Unions of Success</note>. 

On **Tuesday** (Bhauma), the stars Vishakha and Bharani, as well as the 7th, 12th, and 14th lunar days, are noted. If the 14th lunar day coincides with Shravana, Pushya, Anuradha, Rohini, or Mrigashira, or if the 10th or 9th lunar days occur, these are also declared as **Siddhi-yogas**. 74 

On **Monday** (Soma), the stars Purva Ashadha, Uttara Ashadha, Swati, Chitra, and Vishakha, along with the 11th, 6th, and 12th lunar days, should be avoided <note>these are "Opposed" unions</note>. 75 

On **Wednesday** (Budha), the 3rd, 8th, and 6th lunar days are declared as **Siddhi-yogas** by the wise. The stars Uttara Ashadha, Bhadra <note>likely Shravana</note>, Ardra, and Dhanishtha should be observed. 72 On **Tuesday** (Bhauma), the 2nd and 8th should be avoided with effort. 

On **Wednesday** (Budha), the stars Pushya, Anuradha, Krittika, Rohini, and Mrigashira, along with the 2nd, 7th, and 12th lunar days, bestow auspiciousness. However, the stars Dhanishtha, Bharani, Mula, Ashvini, and Revati, as well as the 3rd, 1st, and 9th lunar days, are known as "Opposed" (Viruddha). 

On **Thursday** (Guru), the stars Pushya, Anuradha, Vishakha, Ashvini, Punarvasu, and Revati, along with the 10th, 5th, or the Full Moon, are auspicious. On **Thursday**, the 6th and 8th lunar days, or the 4th with the star Shatabhisha, Krittika, or Uttara Phalguni, should be avoided.

On **Friday** (Shukra), the stars Ashvini, Chitra, Revati, Punarvasu, and Shravana, along with the 1st, 6th, and 11th lunar days, are "Success Unions." On **Friday** (Bhargava), the stars Rohini, Jyeshtha, Punarvasu, and Magha, as well as the 2nd and 7th lunar days, are "Opposed" in all works. 

On **Saturday** (Shani), the stars Swati, Shravana, both Phalgunis, Rohini, and Mrigashira, along with the 4th, 9th, or 14th lunar days, are "Success Unions." The stars Purva Ashadha, Uttara Ashadha, Chitra, Hasta, Revati, and Uttara Phalguni...

### Now, the Chapter on Specific Prohibited Combinations <note>Sajya-prakaranam</note>

The faults <note>doshas</note> arising from the <term>Tithi</term> <note>lunar day</note>, <term>Nakshatra</term> <note>lunar mansion</note>, and weekday, as well as the conjunctions of the "Evil Yogas" <note>inauspicious mathematical points in the sky</note> like Vyatipata and others, are described here. 5 One’s birth star, lunar day, and month are particularly afflicted when joined with malefic planets <note>such as Mars or Saturn</note>; this is known as the "pierced" <note>viddha</note> or "shaken head" <note>ladita-mastaka</note> state. 1

The influence of portents or a solar/lunar eclipse lasts for six months, three months, or one month depending on the severity. 2 Regarding travel, one should avoid starting a journey for seven days following an eclipse. 4 When the Sun and Moon reside together in the same sign, one should stay put; this creates an "affliction" <note>patana</note> that is seen in various ways and through many examples. 6

The Moon’s influence in Aries or its movement through the birth sign and the eighth sign from the birth <term>Lagna</term> <note>the sign rising on the eastern horizon</note> must be considered. One day at the end of a month or half a <term>ghatika</term> <note>a unit of 24 minutes</note> at the end of a Tithi should be avoided. 8

The "Visti" <note>Bhadra; a specific period considered unlucky for new ventures</note>, the "One-Axe" <note>Ekargala</note> fault, and the "Burnt Days" <note>Dagda-tithi</note> are easily understood as times of low energy. 9 At midday, human activity is hindered by these fruits; so too during an intercalary month <note>Malamasa; an extra month added to the lunar calendar</note> or when Venus or Jupiter are "combust" <note>too close to the sun to be visible</note>. 10

One's birth sign should be peaceful; if the star is afflicted, the body may suffer from disease. If these factors are auspicious, success is achieved.

-> Thus concludes the Chapter on **Specific Prohibited Combinations** in the *Muhurta Ganapati*. <-

### Now, the Chapter on the Rising Signs <note>Lagna-prakaranam</note>

The zodiac signs are, in order: Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius, and Pisces. These signs are further classified as **Movable** <note>Chara</note>, **Fixed** <note>Sthira</note>, and **Dual** <note>Dvisvabhava</note> in repeating sequence.

Sagittarius, Aries, Capricorn, Scorpio, and Cancer are known as "Rising from the Rear" <note>Prishtodaya; signs that emerge hind-part first over the horizon</note>. Pisces and others are "Rising from Both Ends" <note>Ubhayodaya</note>. The others rise by the head <note>Shirshodaya</note>. Aries, Taurus, Sagittarius, Gemini, Cancer, and Capricorn are strong at night. The remaining signs are strong during the day. 5

**The Lords of the Signs:**
Mars <note>Bhauma</note> is the lord of Aries and Scorpio. Mercury <note>Jña</note> rules Gemini and Virgo. Venus <note>Shukra</note> rules Libra and Taurus. The Moon rules Cancer. 5 The Sun is the lord of Leo. Saturn rules Capricorn and Aquarius. Jupiter <note>Jiva</note> rules Sagittarius and Pisces. These are remembered as the Lords of the Signs. 9

**Exaltation of Planets:**
Aries, Taurus, Capricorn, Virgo, Cancer, Pisces, and Libra are the signs where the Sun and the other planets, in their respective order, are said to be **Exalted** <note>Uccha; the position where a planet's influence is strongest</note>. 7

**The Degrees of Deep Exaltation:**
For the Sun and others, the degrees of deep exaltation are: 10, 3, 28, 15, 5, 27, and 20. 8 The "Debilitation" <note>Nicha; the weakest point</note> for the Sun and others is the seventh sign from their exaltation sign. The "Moolatrikona" <note>a planet's favorite sign, slightly less powerful than exaltation</note> for the Sun is Leo, for the Moon is Taurus, for Mars is Aries, for Mercury is Virgo, for Jupiter is Sagittarius, for Venus is Libra, and for Saturn is Aquarius. 9

Generally, these are auspicious. 10. Now, regarding activities in the **Ascendant of Aries and others**: **Bold actions, dealing with land, vehicles, and royal consecrations** <note>original: "bhumiyānabhiṣecane"</note> are suitable; it is well known that these succeed when the Ascendant is in Aries. 11. Farming, agriculture, giving gifts, and selecting a bride <note>original: "kumārī-varaṇe"</note> should be done when **Taurus** is rising. 12. ### The Seven Subdivisions of the Signs <note>Sapta-varga</note>

**The Sign <note>Rashi</note>, the Hour <note>Hora</note>, the Third <note>Drekkana</note>, the Ninth <note>Navamsha</note>, the Twelfth <note>Dwadashamsha</note>, and the Thirtieth <note>Trimshamsha</note>**—these are known as the divisions. 24. **A "Hora" is half of a sign, a "Drekkana" is a third, a "Navamsha" is a ninth part, a "Dwadashamsha" is a twelfth part, and a "Trimshamsha" is a thirtieth part.** The planet that rules the sign is the lord of that Ascendant. 25. **In an odd sign, the Horas are ruled by the Sun and Moon respectively; in an even sign, it is the reverse. The lords of the Drekkanas are the rulers of the 1st, 5th, and 9th signs from that position.** 26. **For Aries, Leo, and Sagittarius, the Ninth-parts (Navamshas) begin with Aries. For Capricorn, Taurus, and Virgo, they are said to begin with Capricorn. For Libra, Gemini, and Aquarius, they begin with Libra** <margin>hand</margin> **by the wise. For Cancer, Scorpio, and Pisces, they are known to begin with Cancer.** 28. **The Twelfth-parts (Dwadashamshas) are to be known sequentially starting from the sign itself.** In odd signs, the qualities are as stated. 29. **The Thirtieth-parts (Trimshamshas) for odd signs are ruled by Mars, Saturn, Jupiter, Mercury, and Venus.** In even signs, the order is reversed. 31.

## **Now begins the chapter on the timing for activities such as wearing [new] garments and ornaments.**

In the lunar mansions of Hasta, the three Uttaras <note>Uttaraphalguni, Uttarashadha, and Uttarabhadrapada</note>, Pushya, Dhanishtha, both Revatis, Ashvini, Punarvasu, and Rohini, and on auspicious lunar days. On the days of Friday and Jupiter <note>Thursday</note>, the first wearing of new garments is recommended.
The wearing of gold, gems, and ornaments is also auspicious during the previously mentioned lunar mansions <note>term: "Nakshatras"</note> and days. According to the wise, garments may be worn on Wednesday, and even on the days of Mars <note>Tuesday</note> or the Sun <note>Sunday</note> [under specific conditions]. 3. Now, the results of the days: wearing old or worn clothes on the day of the Sun leads to fire; on the day of the Moon, it is auspicious; on the day of Mars, it leads to destruction; on Wednesday, it brings wealth; on Thursday, it brings wisdom and knowledge; on Friday, it brings beloved friends; and on Saturday, the clothes will quickly become soiled. 4.
The results of wearing new clothes in various lunar mansions: in Ashvini, it brings many clothes; in Bharani, it brings destruction. 5. In Krittika, it causes fear; in Rohini, it brings all types of wealth. In Mrigashira, there is a fear of mice <note>i.e., the clothes being chewed or destroyed</note>; in Ardra, it leads to death or loss. 6. In Punarvasu and Pushya, there are great festivals of wealth and virtue. In Ashlesha, there will be sorrow and the certain destruction of the garment. 7. In Magha, there is fear from the king; in Purvaphalguni, there is an arrival of wealth. Success in actions is found in Hasta, and victory and desired riches are also attained. 8.
In Chitra, one receives fine food; in Svati and Vishakha, there is a growth of intelligence. Friends are gained in Anuradha, but in Jyeshtha, there is delusion regarding the garment. 9. In Mula and Purvashadha, there is danger of water or disease. Fine food is gained in Uttarashadha, but in Shravana, there may be eye afflictions. 10. There is an arrival of grain in Dhanishtha, and fear of obstacles in Shatabhisha. For all causes of gain... <margin>15</margin>

<meta>This page continues the discussion on Muhurta (auspicious timing), moving from the general astrological houses described on the previous page to specific daily activities. Specifically, it focuses on the timing for personal grooming, fashion, and the maintenance of household items like clothing and footwear.</meta>

### On the Wearing of New Clothes and Ornaments

The arrival of the marks of battle <note>original: "lāñchītir-uttarāyodhana-āgamaḥ"; this may refer to the specific markings or insignias worn during certain ceremonies or conflicts</note>. || 11 The wearing of clothes should be accompanied by gem-encrusted ornaments. Now, for the specific clothing of women: the lunar mansions <note>term: "Nakshatra" — one of 27 sectors of the sky along the moon's path</note> of **Ashvini** and **Dhanishtha**, as well as the three **Uttaras** <note>Uttara Phalguni, Uttara Ashadha, and Uttara Bhadrapada</note>. || 12

Gold, precious gems, and fine jewelry should be worn by women when they first don their clothes. The constellations of **Chitra**, **Hasta**, **Dhanishtha**, **Revati**, and **Mrigashira** are auspicious. If these fall on a Friday <note>term: "Shukra-vara"</note>, it is said to be highly beneficial for women. Under **Rohini**, the **Uttaras**, **Pushya**, or **Punarvasu**, garments should be donned at an appropriate time. || 13

Kings and those of high status should know that many benefits come from wearing new garments under these stars. However, if one wears clothes that are burnt, worn out, or soiled, it brings misfortune <note>the text implies a spiritual or energetic "stain" from neglected clothing</note>. || 14 It should be known that under a benevolent sign, the wearing of clothes brings goodness to all members of the household. || 17

For the wearing of gem-studded clothes at weddings, festivals, and the like, as well as for those seeking victory in knowledge or offering food to the gods, these timings are beloved by all. || 18

### On the Specifics of Fabrics and Colors

Now, for the distinctions among garments: white clothes are recommended on **Arka** <note>Sunday, the day of the Sun</note> and Monday. When the moon is in **Pushya** or the two **Uttaras**, white is best. On **Bhauma** <note>Tuesday, the day of Mars</note>, red garments are superior. On a Wednesday, yellow garments are favored. || 19

Under **Punarvasu** and **Dhanishtha**, red is auspicious. Under the previously mentioned stars, blue or black garments are considered favorable for certain works. Now, if one wears a garment for the first time on a Thursday <note>term: "Guru-vara"</note> when the planet is in a fixed sign, and stays in the home with friends, it brings prosperity. || 21

### On Specialized Materials

Now regarding **Kausheya** <note>term: "Kausheya" — silk, specifically wild silk or "cocoon-born" fabric</note>: it should be worn under **Ashvini**, **Revati**, **Hasta**, the three **Shravanas**, the **Uttaras**, **Punarvasu**, **Pushya**, or **Magha**. This is the method for the joining of silk garments. 

Now for **Romaja** <note>term: "Romaja" — woolen clothing, literally "born of hair"</note>: blue woolen garments are auspicious under the constellations of **Aditi** <note>Punarvasu</note>, **Revati**, and **Magha**, particularly on a Friday. || 23

### On Sewing and Crafting

Now for the wearing of the **Kanchuka** <note>term: "Kanchuka" — a fitted bodice, tunic, or armored jacket</note>: the stars **Pushya**, the **Uttaras**, **Anuradha**, **Dhanishtha**, **Hasta**, **Chitra**, and **Swati** are preferred. When the moon is in **Rohini**, and the day is ruled by Jupiter, Venus, or Mercury, and the star is **Ashvini** or **Magha**, it is very auspicious. || 24

One should wear clothes adorned with gold and gems under **Magha** or **Vayu** <note>Swati</note> on a Sunday. This brings a fortunate result. Now, for the manufacture of clothing: **Rohini**, **Revati**, **Chitra**, **Anuradha**, and **Mrigashira**. When Saturn is well-placed or on a Tuesday, one may work with copper or other metallic tools for the task. || 25

For the dyeing of clothes <note>original: "rañjanam"</note>: under the stars **Punarvasu**, **Hasta**, and **Swati**, the coloring of white cloth into various shades is auspicious, especially on the days of the Sun, Venus, or Mars. || 27

### On Needlework and Cleaning

Now for **Suchi-karma** <note>term: "Suchi-karma" — needlework, sewing, or tailoring</note>: **Mrigashira**, **Ashvini**, **Anuradha**, and **Pushya** are recommended. 

<margin>14</margin> **Rohini**, **Hasta**, **Jyeshtha**, **Magha**, and the solar days are also approved for needlework. || 28

Now for the washing of clothes <note>original: "vastra-chalane"</note>: under **Punarvasu** or **Ashvini**, on the sixth or eighth lunar days, one should not wash clothes using alkaline substances <note>original: "ajakādina," referring to goat dung or alkaline ashes used as soap in antiquity</note> during a religious vow. || 29

### On Footwear and Leatherwork

Now regarding **Upanat-paridhana** <note>term: "Upanat" — sandals or shoes</note> and leatherwork: the stars **Chitra**, the two **Vishakhas**, **Bharani**, **Jyeshtha**, **Hasta**, **Magha**, **Mrigashira**, **Vishakha**, **Krittika**, **Mula**, and **Revati** are favorable. Under the influence of Saturn and the Sun, one should perform the wearing of shoes or tasks involving leather. For the making of mattresses, cushions, or cloth-walled structures <note>such as tents or pavilions</note>, one should bind them under the star **Mula**. || 32

Now for the construction of houses made of cloth and the like: use the stars **Punarvasu**, **Pushya**, **Anuradha**, and **Rohini**.

### On the Use of Vessels

Under the three **Shravanas** <note>original: "shravana-tritaye"; refers to the constellations Shravana, Dhanishtha, and Shatabhisha</note>, **Pushya**, **Punarvasu**, and **Anuradha**, as well as the three **Uttara** constellations. On a Wednesday, Friday, or Thursday <note>original: "Budha-Shukra-Jya-vare"</note>, the "Nectar Union" <note>term: "Amrita-yoga" — a highly auspicious astrological alignment</note> is formed. At such times, the use of gold and silver vessels for eating and other activities is highly auspicious.

### On Grooming and Household Items

<margin>18</margin>
Under **Mrigashira**, the three **Hastas** <note>Hasta, Chitra, and Svati</note>, **Punarvasu**, and the **Uttara** stars, the arrangement of cloth and the home is favorable. || 32

**Now, the Union for Perfumes:**
Under **Shravana**, **Ashvini**, **Pushya**, **Purva Ashadha**, **Anuradha**, the three **Hastas**, **Punarvasu**, and **Mrigashira**, on an auspicious day, || 33 the wearing of sandalwood, aguru <note>aloeswood</note>, musk, and flowers is auspicious.

**Now, the Union for Beds and Seats:**
Under **Shravana**, **Anuradha**, **Mrigashira**, **Rohini**, **Punarvasu**, **Hasta**, **Vishakha**, and the three **Revatis**, is the union for preparing beds, seats, and similar furniture, provided it falls on a favorable day of the week and a favorable lunar day <note>term: "Tithi" — a lunar day in the Hindu calendar</note>. || 36

**Now, for Ornaments and Wealth:**
In the "Triple Power" union <note>original: "Trishakti-abhidha-yoge"</note>, under **Mrigashira**, **Revati**, **Ashvini** <note>original: "Haye"</note>, the three **Shravanas**, **Pushya**, **Punarvasu**, **Anuradha**, the three **Hastas**, and **Rohini**, the crafting and wearing of jewelry should be performed on a fine day.

**Now, for Gem-Set Jewelry:**
Under the three stars beginning with **Krittika** <note>Krittika, Rohini, Mrigashira</note>, the five **Hastas**, **Revati**, **Ashvini**, the three **Shravanas**, **Punarvasu**, **Pushya**, and the three **Uttaras**, the creation of ornaments set with various gems is favored on auspicious days.

**Now, the Fashioning of Beds, Pearls, and Jewelry:**
For the creation of gem-set ornaments, excluding **Vishakha** and **Krittika**, if it be a Friday or Wednesday, jewelry made of silver, diamonds, and pearls is indeed excellent. || 4

### On Personal Grooming and Shaving

**Now, the Enjoyment of Vessels and Grooming:**
Under the two **Rohinis**, the three **Hastas**, the two **Revatis**, the three **Shravanas**, **Jyeshtha**, **Mrigashira**, and the two **Punarvasus**, <term>Kshura-karma</term> <note>The ritual act of shaving or hair-cutting</note> is prescribed by the wise. Men should avoid Tuesday, Saturday, and Sunday. Also to be avoided are **Anuradha**, **Phalguni**, **Krittika**, **Rohini**, and **Magha**. || 45

Regarding the results of the days: Sunday, Tuesday, and Saturday are like fire; they consume one's lifespan. The fifth day <note>Thursday</note> increases wealth. The Moon <note>Monday</note> is favorable. || 48 Friday and Wednesday are beneficial, and Jupiter <note>Thursday</note> is excellent.

**Now, the Forbidden Lunar Days:**
The 8th, 1st, and 6th <note>Ashtami, Pratipat, Shashthi</note> should be abandoned. The 14th <note>Chaturdashi</note> and the New Moon <note>Amavasya</note> are to be avoided entirely for these activities.

**Now, the results for specific stars:**
[The frequency of grooming is restricted by the stars:] Under **Krittika** six times, under **Anuradha** three times, under **Rohini** eight times, under **Uttara Phalguni** seven times, and under **Chitra** four times one may practice hair-cutting. || 25

Whether one is a priest or a commoner, in times of sickness or when offering food to the gods, one must observe these rules. Under the proper <term>Kshaurabha</term> <note>another term for the shaving ritual</note>, happiness comes to kings. If <term>Smribhu-karma</term> <note>shaving/grooming</note> is performed on the fifth day, it is well-said; but if done when forbidden, one may fall into a hellish state. || 50
Even when the wise permit hair-cutting, the rituals of cleansing and bathing must still be performed. || 51

**Now, the Prohibitions on Hair-cutting:**
One should not undergo hair-cutting after eating, after an oil massage, while hungry, when in a state of passion, if one is a yogi, or while performing daily rites. Under the stars **Uttara**, **Bharani**, or **Raudra** <note>Ardra</note>, and during both the morning and evening twilights. On the 9th day, hair-cutting should not be done. This applies to students, kings, yogis, and the sick. Even if it is a "Life-Giving" <note>Sanjivani</note> day, these prohibitions mentioned before regarding the twilights must be observed. For hair-cutting to bring auspiciousness... <margin>19</margin>

...Diti, Aditi <note>original: "diti maditiṃca"; these are the deities associated with the constellations Punarvasu and others</note>, and the constellation **Hasta**; these lead to welfare. 57 In **Mrigashira** and the three stars of **Ashvini**, one gains benefits. **Beginning the Study of Mathematical Texts:**
In the two hundred <note>likely referring to a verse number or a specific section of a larger work</note>, under **Anuradha**, **Rohini**, **Revati**, **Hasta**, and **Pushya**, and on the days of Jupiter or Mercury, a person should begin the study of mathematics and calculations. 

**On Beginning Grammar:**
A wise person should study Grammar under **Rohini**, the five stars beginning with **Hasta**, **Punarvasu**, **Shravana**, and **Mrigashira**, or in **Pushya**, on a Friday or Wednesday. 58 

**On Beginning the Study of Logic and Philosophy:**
One should study the Science of Logic <note>term: "Nyaya-shastra" — the Indian school of philosophy focused on logic and epistemology</note> and other philosophical sciences under the **Uttara** stars, **Rohini**, **Pushya**, **Punarvasu**, **Dhanishtha**, **Ashvini**, **Shatabhisha**, and **Svati**. 61 

Under the five stars beginning with **Hasta**, **Pushya**, **Revati**, **Mrigashira**, and the three "Fixed" stars <note>term: "Dhruva" — referring to the stable constellations: the three Uttaras and Rohini</note>, it is a favorable beginning for the study of Religious Law and the ancient histories. <note>original: "Dharmashastra Purana"</note> 

**On Beginning the Study of Medicine and the Arts of Garuda:**
Under the three **Hastas**, **Anuradha**, the three **Punarvasu-Shravana** stars, **Mula**, **Ashvini**, and **Pushya**, it is auspicious to hear or study medicine. 62 
When beginning the study of medicine, or the Garudi arts <note>term: "Garudi-vidya" — traditional lore related to the curing of snakebites and mystical spells associated with the celestial eagle Garuda</note>, it should be done on a Wednesday or Sunday. One should begin the study of the Agamas <note>term: "Agama" — a collection of scriptures containing technical instructions for rituals, philosophy, and cosmology</note> under **Shravana**, **Magha**, the **Purva** and **Uttara** stars, **Revati**, and **Svati**, on a Sunday, Friday, or Wednesday. 63 

**On Beginning the Study of Persian and Foreign Lexicons:**
Under **Jyeshtha**, **Ashlesha**, **Magha**, **Purva**, **Revati**, the two **Bharanis**, **Vishakha**, **Purva Ashadha**, and **Shatabhisha**, on a "malefic" day <note>term: "Papa-vasara" — typically Tuesday or Saturday, usually avoided but here specified for foreign or "harsh" languages</note>, 64 with the Moon in a "fixed" rising sign, one should study the Persian language <note>original: "Parasi"</note>. 

**On Beginning the Study of Writing:**
On an auspicious lunar day and an auspicious day of the week, under the pair of **Revatis** 65, **Shravana**, **Anuradha**, and the three **Ashvinis**, or the three stars starting with **Hasta**, a wise person should begin the practice of writing. 

**On the Examination of Jewels:**
Under **Punarvasu** and the two **Hastas**, for those seeking to enter a profession, the examination of gems is recommended on the eighth lunar day, avoiding Tuesday and Saturday. 

**On Beginning the Study of Crafts:**
Under the three **Hastas**, the three **Shravanas**, the **Uttara** stars, **Rohini**, **Mrigashira**, **Revati**, **Ashvini**, **Pushya**, **Punarvasu**, and **Anuradha**, 69 on a Saturday or another auspicious day of the week, one should practice the arts of craftsmanship and architecture <note>term: "Shilpa-vidya" — encompasses arts, crafts, engineering, and sculpture</note>. 

**On Meeting the King:**
Meeting a king or high official is prescribed under the **Uttara** stars, the two **Shravanas**, **Mrigashira**, **Pushya**, **Anuradha**, **Rohini**, the two **Revatis**, **Chitra**, and **Hasta**, on a Sunday or when the Sun is strong.

### On the Hoisting of Flags

Now, the making and hoisting of flags should be done under the constellations of **Mrigashirsha**, **Anuradha**, **Pushya**, the three stars of **Shravana** <note>Shravana, Dhanishta, and Shatabhisha</note>, the two **Punarvasus**, the three **Hastas**, or the pair of **Revatis**. 74 One should also look for **Mrigashirsha** or **Rohini** on the 8th or the middle day <note>referring to the 15th/Full Moon</note>. The 3rd, 12th, or the Full Moon lunar days are also favorable. 75 Avoiding the solar transitions <note>term: "Ayana" — the movement of the sun north or south</note>, activities performed on Monday or Wednesday grant success in both spiritual and worldly goals.

### Riding Elephants, Horses, and Chariots

The first riding of elephants, horses, or chariots, and the first entry into a village should be done under the pair of **Revatis**, the three **Hastas**, the three **Shravanas**, **Mrigashira**, or the two **Punarvasus**. This should be performed on days other than Tuesday or Sunday <note>original: "vibhaumarka-vasare"</note>. 76 The enjoyment of elephants, horses, chariots, or villages should occur on an auspicious lunar day.

### On the Use of Palanquins

Regarding the riding of palanquins <note>term: "Shivika" — a covered litter carried by people</note>: **Swati**, **Krittika**, the three **Uttaras**, the two **Revatis**, the three **Hastas**, **Magha**, **Punarvasu**, **Pushya**, **Anuradha**, **Mrigashira**, and **Rohini** are the constellations to be used in their proper order.

### Rituals and Tasks for Elephants

The activities involving elephants <note>original: "gaja-kritya"</note> should be performed under the three stars of **Shravana**, the three of **Hasta**, or the two of **Revati**. 18 Also under **Mrigashirsha**, **Pushya**, **Anuradha**, **Rohini**, the **Uttaras**, and the two **Punarvasus**. On a favorable weekday, one should direct these auspicious works. 8 

**The Taming of Elephants:**
On a favorable day, in an auspicious rising sign <note>term: "Lagna" — the zodiac sign on the eastern horizon</note> or an auspicious division of a sign <note>term: "Amsha"</note>, and on a beautiful lunar day, even fierce female elephants can be fed or tamed. This is especially favored when the rising sign or the day is ruled by Saturn. 21

### Tasks and Equipment for Horses

Now, regarding the activities for horses: they are auspicious on a good day under **Ashlesha**, **Dhanishta**, the pair of **Revatis**, the two **Punarvasus**, **Swati**, **Mrigashirsha**, and the star named **Revati** <note>original: "dhanabhidhe"</note>. 22 Avoiding the "empty" lunar days <note>term: "Rikta-tithi" — the 4th, 9th, and 14th days of the lunar fortnight, considered unfavorable for new starts</note> and Tuesday, horse-related work brings prosperity.

<margin>Pu 4</margin>**Construction of Saddles and Gear:**
The creation of saddles and other equipment is auspicious under the three **Shravanas**, **Hasta**, **Pushya**, **Mula**, and **Mrigashira**. <gloss>g</gloss> The making of saddles for elephants and horses is best under these stars. The rules for the first tonsure <note>original: "chaula"</note> and other rituals also apply to the first time these animals are fitted with gear or fed, as mentioned in the peace-rituals for horses. 26

### The Use of Chariots

Now, the rituals for chariots <note>original: "ratha-kritya"</note>: these should be done under **Pushya**, **Punarvasu**, <margin>2</margin> **Anuradha**, the pair of **Revatis**, the three **Shravanas**, the three **Hastas**, **Rohini**, and **Mrigashira**. 27 On a Sunday or Wednesday, actions associated with the "Lord of Wealth" <note>likely referring to Kubera or the planet Mercury</note> are successful.

### Weapons and Archery

Regarding the storage of bows: under **Krittika**, **Hasta**, and **Vishakha**, on a Tuesday, Sunday, or Saturday, and in a favorable rising sign, the protection of the armory brings victory.

**Bearing Weapons:**
Under the two **Punarvasus**, **Hasta**, **Vishakha**, the two **Rohinis**, the three **Vishakhas**, the **Uttaras**, and the two **Revatis**. 80 One should avoid the "empty" lunar days. On a Friday or Thursday, one may take up armor, daggers, or other weapons.

**Beginning the Study of Archery:**
The study of archery <note>term: "Dhanur-vidya"</note> and similar martial arts should be started under **Anuradha**, **Magha**, **Pushya**, or **Mrigashirsha**, on an auspicious lunar day like the 12th, and on a favorable weekday. 88 Under the three **Hastas**, the **Uttaras**, **Shravana**, **Punarvasu**, or **Pushya**, on an auspicious day, the wise should begin practicing with the bow and other weapons. 80

### On the Wearing of Weapons

Regarding the wearing of weapons and **the use of firearms** <note>original: "nalika-agni-vastra"; literally "tube-fire-garment/tool," referring to early cannons or matchlocks</note> <gloss>ka</gloss>: The constellations **Ashlesha**, **Krittika**, **Mula**, **Jyeshtha**, **Ardra**, **Bharani**, and **Magha** 92, along with the **Purva** stars, **Hasta**, and the fourth and final **Punarvasu**, are recommended. The ritual for tube-fire weapons <note>original: "nalika-astra-vidhi"</note> is auspicious on the "Void" <note>term: "Rikta" — the 4th, 9th, and 14th lunar days, usually considered unfavorable for most tasks but suitable for weaponry</note> and "Victory" <note>term: "Jaya" — the 3rd, 8th, and 13th lunar days</note> lunar days, even on otherwise "evil" days. 93

### On Striking and Piercing the Enemy

**Regarding the piercing and striking of enemies**: Under the stars **Jyeshtha**, **Ardra**, **Bharani**, the **Purva** group, **Ashlesha**, and **Magha**, when cruel planets are in the ascendant <note>original: "krura-kheta"</note> or on days associated with harshness 94, one should perform the piercing of the ranks or the striking with whips and other weapons.

### On the Immobilization of Enemies

**Regarding enemy immobilization**: Under **Anuradha**, **Magha**, and **Pushya** <margin>घ ५</margin>, or the star known as **Tishya** 95, when **Rahu** or **Shikhi** <note>the lunar nodes Rahu and Ketu</note> are in the ascendant and well-placed, the ritual for binding an enemy is desired.

### Commencing a First Campaign

**The first departure for war**: Under **Mula**, **Ardra**, **Shravana**, **Jyeshtha**, **Vishakha**, and **Magha** 96, or **Bharani**, on the days of "cruel" planets <note>such as Tuesday or Saturday</note>, the wise perform activities related to battle <unclear>n</unclear>.

### On the Consumption of Lethal Substances

**Regarding the consumption of poisonous or lethal substances**: On **Ardra**, **Ashlesha**, **Magha**, the **Purvas**, **Jyeshtha**, **Mula**, and **Shatabhisha** 97, or **Bharani**, on a favorable day, one may administer lethal substances to those intended.

### On the Enjoyment of New Songs

**Regarding the first enjoyment of new songs**: The first performance or encounter should occur during the night when the moon is bright and the internal timing <note>term: "Karana" — an astrological period half the length of a lunar day</note> is favorable.

### On the Practice of Singing and Music

**Regarding the beginning of singing**: In **Revati**, **Anuradha**, the two stars starting with **Dhanishta**, **Hasta**, the pair of **Rohinis**, and **Pushya**, singing and dancing are auspicious. 99

**Regarding the work of dancers**: For those who live by performance <note>original: "samhara-jivina"; likely referring to actors or traveling entertainers</note>, the stars **Mrigashirsha**, **Ardra**, **Rohini**, **Pushya**, **Punarvasu**, the three **Shravanas**, **Chitra**, the **Uttaras**, and **Mula** are favorable for their work. 100

### On Hand Drums like the Dandibha and Mridanga

In the three **Hastas**, the end of **Anuradha**, the pair of **Punarvasus**, the three **Shravanas**, **Mrigashirsha**, and the "sun" stars, on the "Victory" <note>Jaya</note> days, the playing of hand-drums such as the **Dandibha** <note>a small, rattle-like drum</note> and the **Mridanga** <note>a classic double-headed barrel drum</note> is recommended.

### On Flutes and Wind Instruments

Under the auspicious **Jyeshtha**, **Ardra**, **Bharani**, **Mula**, the **Purvas**, **Ashlesha**, and **Magha**, on the "Void" <note>Rikta</note> lunar days or even on "evil" days, the practice of wind instruments <note>original: "mukha-vadya"; literally "mouth-instruments" like flutes or trumpets</note> is praised.

### On the Hunt

Under **Ashlesha**, **Bharani**, the **Purvas**, **Jyeshtha**, **Ardra**, **Swati**, **Mula**, and **Vishakha**, a king or hunter may go out for the hunt <note>original: "mrigaya"</note> 103.

### On Waterworks and Hydraulic Systems

**Regarding the construction of water engines and channels**: Under **Purvashadha**, **Magha**, **Ashlesha**, and **Pushya**, when the ascendant is in a "watery" sign and the day is favorable, the creation of water engines <note>term: "Jala-yantra" — could refer to fountains, irrigation wheels, or water clocks</note> is auspicious. 104

**Regarding the beginning of step-wells, wells, and ponds**: Under **Anuradha**, **Magha**, **Hasta**, **Revati**, the pair of **Rohinis**, **Pushya**, and the two **Dhanishtas** 105, or **Purvashadha**, during an auspicious month and day, the construction of step-wells <note>original: "vapi"</note>, wells <note>original: "kupa"</note>, and ponds <note>original: "tadaga"</note> is proclaimed by the wise. 107 Under **Hasta**, **Chitra**, the **Uttaras**, **Mula**, **Anuradha**, the two **Revatis**, **Vishakha**, **Rohini**, and **Pushya**, these projects will reach their completion. 108

### On Boat-Building

**Regarding the work of boats**: Avoiding **Jyeshtha**, **Magha**, **Vishakha**, **Ardra**, **Rohini**, the two **Bharanis**, and **Ashlesha**, and using other stars when the Sun, Jupiter, or Venus are in a good position 109, the construction and launching of a boat on a good lunar day is auspicious.

**Regarding general activities for livestock**: On the 4th, 8th, or 14th lunar days, and under **Rohini** and the three **Uttaras** 110, **Vishakha**, and **Shravana**, on a Tuesday, rituals for animals...

### On the Training of Birds

Now, regarding actions for birds on auspicious days: for the training and care of **parrots**, **mynahs**, and **hawks**, the stars **Jyeshtha** <note>original: "Aindra"</note> and **Vishakha** <note>original: "Suradhipa"</note> are best. ### On the Trade of Livestock

Now, for the **buying and selling of animals**: the constellations **Vishakha**, **Revati**, **Pushya**, **Dhanishtha**, **Shatabhisha**, **Hasta**, **Punarvasu**, and **Jyeshtha** are recommended for the commercial exchange of livestock. 

### On the Sale of General Goods

Now, for the **sale of all household items and goods**: the stars **Vishakha**, **Krittika**, **Ashlesha**, **Bharani**, and the three **Purva** stars <note>referring to Purva Phalguni, Purva Ashadha, and Purva Bhadrapada</note>. 120 Sale is recommended during "conjunctions" <note>original: "sannipateshu"</note>, but buying goods at such a time is not considered auspicious. 

### On the Purchase and Sale of Land and Laborers

Now, for the **buying and selling of houses, fields, and laborers**: the best times are on **Thursdays** (Jupiter), under the star **Pushya**, or on **Tuesdays** (Mars). The stars **Mrigashira**, **Mula**, the **Purva** stars, and the two **Vishakhas** are also favorable. 129 Thus it was declared by the sages that the buying and selling of land <note>original: "bhuvah"</note> should occur under **Punarvasu**, **Anuradha**, the **Uttara** stars, **Pushya**, **Revati**, **Rohini**, and **Mrigashira**.

### On the Finding and Storing of Treasure

### On Financial Transactions and Debt

Under the stars **Shravana**, **Punarvasu** <note>original: "Aditi-bhe"</note>, the group of four stars beginning with **Chitra**, the two **Revatis**, **Mrigashirsha**, and **Pushya**, the lending of goods or money is auspicious. <unclear>27</unclear>. During a **Sankranti** <term>Sankranti — the moment the sun moves into a new zodiac sign</term>, or during an "Increase" alignment <term>Vriddhi-yoga — a specific astrological period thought to multiply results</term>, or under the **Hasta** stars, one should not take a loan. This is because the debt taken then will never remain stable <note>meaning the person will struggle to remain financially steady or be trapped in debt</note>. 24. 

The repayment of debt should be performed on a **Tuesday** <note>original: "Kuja"; the day of Mars, considered fierce and suitable for "cutting" or ending things</note>. However, money should never be given as a loan on a **Wednesday**.

### On the Diagram of the Plow

<insert>Now, the Plow Diagram.</insert> From the star occupied by the Sun, count the stars in a sequence of two, six, nine, and eight <note>original: "hala-cakram"; an astrological diagram where stars are mapped onto the shape of a plow to predict agricultural success</note>. In this **Plow Diagram**, the auspiciousness or inauspiciousness of the stars should be known in order.

On a favorable day and lunar date, the sowing of seeds is best, provided one avoids the stars associated with **Rahu** <note>the lunar node, considered a shadow planet that brings ill luck to new beginnings</note>.

<meta>This page continues the astrological guide (Muhurta) for various life activities. Regarding three-fold or two-fold actions performed for merit <note>original: "punye"; deeds that generate spiritual or karmic credit</note>, they are celebrated by the wise. In the cycles of the Sun, Mercury, Venus, Saturn, Moon, Mars, and Jupiter, and including the nodes <note>Rahu and Ketu</note>, each governs a set of three stars in sequence. 51. An offering to the fire <note>original: "homahuti"</note> is said to yield fruits; it is destructive if performed under a "harsh" planet and brings good fortune under a "beneficent" planet <term>Khechara — literally 'sky-goer', a term for a planet</term.

### Now, the Results of the Fire’s Residence

Add the number of the lunar day <term>Tithi — there are 30 lunar days in a month</term> and the day of the week, add one, and divide by four. If the remainder indicates the residence of fire is with Mars, Jupiter, or Mercury <note>the text implies a specific calculation for Earthly residence</note>, the residence is auspicious. If the remainder is one, it leads to poverty; if the remainder is two, the ritual becomes fruitless. 52.

### Now, regarding Mantra Initiation

Under the stars **Rohini**, the three **Uttaras** <note>Uttara-Phalguni, Uttara-Ashadha, and Uttara-Bhadrapada</note>, and the stars beginning with the "tying of the girdle" <note>original: "mauji bandhana"; refers to the Upanyana or initiation ceremony</term>, initiation into a mantra <note>original: "mantradiksha"</note> is auspicious. It is also highly recommended during an eclipse as prescribed in the sacred texts <term>Agamas — a collection of scriptures and manuals for worship and ritual</term>. 53. Under the stars **Uttara-Phalguni**, **Hasta**, **Ashvini**, **Krttika**, **Vishakha**, and **Mrigashirsha**, on an auspicious day when these stars are combined with beneficial planets, one should perform rituals involving mantras, mystical diagrams <term>Yantra — geometric symbols used in meditation</term>, and sacred vows. 54. Under **Magha**, **Ardra**, **Bharani**, **Mula**, **Mrigashirsha**, on a Friday when the moon is in the sign of the Water-Vessel <note>Aquarius</note> and in an auspicious house, one may perform the practice of the "Hero's Spirit" <note>original: "vira vetala sadhana"; a specific esoteric or tantric ritual</note>.

### Now, regarding Medicine

Under the three **Hastas**, **Anuradha**, **Mula**, **Pushya**, the three **Shravanas**, **Mrigashirsha**, the two **Revatis**, **Punarvasu**, and **Ashvini** <note>original: "dvijanmabhe"</note>, it is auspicious to prepare or take medicine. 56.

### Now, regarding the Production of Alchemical Elixirs

On the days of the Moon <note>Monday</note>, Venus <note>Friday</note>, and Mercury <note>Wednesday</note>, especially when they are in proximity to each other, under a "dual" zodiac sign <note>Gemini, Virgo, Sagittarius, or Pisces</note> in an auspicious rising sign, the production of alchemical juices <term>Rasa — specifically mercury or potent medicinal mineral preparations</term> should be done. 57. Under the signs of Aries, when combined with an auspicious Moon, or under **Vishakha**, **Krttika**, **Mula**, **Dhanishtha**, **Shatabhisha**, or **Mrigashirsha**, or on the days of Jyeshtha, Ardra, or Mercury, alchemical work <note>original: "rasa kriya"</note> is favored.

### Now, regarding the Consumption of Alchemical Elixirs

Under the three **Hastas**, **Ashvini**, **Pushya**, **Anuradha**, the end of **Revati**, the three **Shravanas**, 58, **Punarvasu**, **Mrigashirsha**, or on the days of the Sun <note>Sunday</note>, Mars <note>Tuesday</note>, or Mercury <note>Wednesday</note>, one should consume these elixirs.

### Now, regarding the Use of Oil for Wind-related Ailments

Leaving aside **Ashvini**, **Magha**, **Mula**, **Vishakha**, **Ardra**, and **Bharani**, one should apply medicinal oils <note>original: "tailam"; likely for massage or internal use to treat "Vata" or wind-imbalance like arthritis</note> on the days of Saturn, Mercury, or the Moon, and on the third lunar day or the following sequence of three. 60.

### Now, regarding Bloodletting, Purgation, and Emesis

Under the three **Hastas**, **Ashvini**, **Pushya**, **Shatabhisha**, the two **Rohinis**, **Shravana**, **Anuradha**, and **Jyeshtha**, bloodletting <note>original: "raktamokshana"</note> should be performed. 61. This should be done on the days of Jupiter, Mars, or the Sun, and on an auspicious lunar day. Purgation <note>original: "vireka"</note> and induced vomiting <note>original: "vamana"</note> should be performed on the day of Venus or the Moon, and under the star **Shatabhisha** <note>original: "varunadi"</note>. 62.

### Now, regarding Cauterization with Hot Iron

Under **Shatabhisha**, **Chitra**, **Ashvini**, **Mula**, **Vishakha**, and **Krttika**, and when the Moon is in **Jyeshtha** or **Hasta**, on the days of Mercury, Mars, or the Sun, or under harsh planetary influences, medical treatments involving fire and iron <note>original: "lohagni bheshajam"; cauterization</note> are prescribed. 64.

### Now, the Duration of Illness Based on the Lunar Mansion

If a fever begins under **Ashvini**, **Krttika**, or **Mula**, it will last for nine days. Under **Rohini**, the two **Uttaras**, **Punarvasu**, or **Pushya**, 65, and **Uttara-Phalguni**, it lasts seven days. Under **Magha**, it lasts twenty days. Under **Shatabhisha**, **Bharani**, **Chitra**, or **Shravana**, the duration is said to be eleven days.

...under **Dhanishta**, **Vishakha**, and **Hasta** as well. Under the month-long influence of **Purva-Ashadha**, **Uttara-Ashadha**, and **Anuradha**, 67. one who is released from pain will truly be happy and without lingering symptoms thereafter.

### Now, the Results of the Onset of Disease

If the onset of a disease occurs under the stars **Ashwini**, **Bharani**, **Mula**, **Swati**, or **Krittika** <note>original: "vahnibhe"; the star of the Fire-god</note>, the death of that person is certain. If one falls ill with a fever under **Ashwini**, **Bharani**, **Mula**, **Swati**, or **Krittika**, or under **Shatabhisha** on a day ruled by a malefic planet <note>such as Tuesday or Saturday</note>, or on the 1st, 12th, 68. 14th, 6th lunar days, or on the Full Moon, that person meets death, deprived of their own wealth. 70.

### Now, Determining the Affliction from the Ascendant at the Onset of Trouble

In **Aries** <note>original: "Mesha"</note>, there is an imbalance of bile <term>Pitta — the biological fire element in Ayurveda</term> and the person may lose their family status. In **Taurus** <note>original: "Vrisha"</note>, the trouble arises from one's personal deity, resulting in bad dreams, eye disease, and fever. 71.

In **Gemini** <note>original: "Dvandva"</note>, the affliction arises from illusion or a fever caused by wind <term>Vata — the air element in Ayurveda</term>. In **Cancer** <note>original: "Karkata"</note>, the affliction is caused by a **Shakini** <note>a type of supernatural female spirit or demoness</note>, leading to strange behavior such as inappropriate laughter or crying. 72. In **Leo** <note>original: "Simha"</note>, the affliction is from a ghost <term>Preta — a spirit of the deceased</term> and the fever settles in the bone marrow. In **Virgo** <note>original: "Kanya"</note>, the trouble is caused by planetary spirits or manifests as intense anger and a loss of appetite. 73.

In **Libra** <note>original: "Tula"</note>, the affliction strikes the children or the lineage. In **Scorpio** <note>original: "Ali"</note>, it is an affliction from the serpent-beings <term>Nagas — semi-divine snake spirits</term>, causing a burning sensation in the body and <unclear>loss of</unclear> intellect. 74. In **Sagittarius** <note>original: "Dhanu"</note>, the body suffers from fear and fever. In **Capricorn** <note>original: "Tu"</note>, it is a ghost affliction; in **Aquarius** <note>original: "Ghata"</note>, it is general bodily pain. 75. In **Pisces** <note>original: "Meena"</note>, the affliction is from a **Yogini** <note>a powerful female spirit or goddess</note>, causing fever and mental confusion. These results should be understood based on whether that sign is the Ascendant <term>Lagna — the zodiac sign rising at the moment of falling ill</term> or the position of the Moon. 76.

### Now, Knowing the Affliction Based on the Planets in the Ascendant

An affliction from the Guardian of the Fields <term>Kshetrapala — a protective local deity</term> occurs if the Sun or Mars is in the Ascendant. An affliction from the Goddess arises if the Moon is in the house of death or the twelfth house. A divine affliction <note>Deva-dosha</note> occurs when Mercury is in the seventh house. If Venus is in the last house or the seventh, the affliction is from the Earth Goddess. 77. A wind-related disease <note>Vata-amaya</note> occurs if Saturn is in the house of friends or the house of loss. If Rahu <note>original: "simhika nandan"; literally the son of the lioness-demoness</note> is in the seventh house, it is a ghost affliction. 80.

If malefic planets are in the 8th, <insert>6th</insert>, or 9th houses from the Ascendant, the affliction belongs to one's own family lineage. Afflictions regarding poison, women, or water are indicated by the lifespan. 81. If the Sun is obscured, or Mars or Saturn are combined with the waves of water <note>water signs</note>, the afflictions are from those who died by drowning. 82.

Mercury in the tenth house suggests an affliction from the Goddess of the Path; Jupiter suggests a ghost. Venus suggests an affliction from one's own personal deity. A malefic planet suggests a **Shakini** spirit. 83. The affliction is from one's own lineage if it occurs in one's own sign; if in the house of friends, it comes from one's own kin.

Where [unclear] is possible: if the Sun is present, there is an affliction; if in the Moon <note>original: "Nishakara"; the night-maker</note>, there is an affliction related to wealth. ### **Now, the Affliction of Snakebite**

If a person is bitten by a snake under the stars **Vishakha**, **Krittika**, **Mula**, or under **Revati** and **Magha**; 88. or if the bite occurs under the name of **Ashlesha**, the victim does not survive. 88.

### **Now, the Bath after Recovery from Illness**

Under the stars **Magha**, **Punarvasu**, **Swati**, **Rohini**, and the three **Uttaras** <note>Uttara Phalguni, Uttara Ashadha, and Uttara Bhadrapada</note>; also under **Ashlesha** and **Revati**, specifically on a Friday <note>original: "Bhargava"</note> or a Monday; 89. one who has been released from illness should NOT bathe when the Moon is in **Aquarius** <note>original: "Kumbha"</note>. Likewise, one should avoid "empty" lunar days <note>Rikta dates: the 4th, 9th, and 14th of the lunar month</note>, Saturdays, Tuesdays, or Sundays, or when a "movable" ascendant <note>Chara Lagna: Aries, Cancer, Libra, or Capricorn</note> is rising. 90. When the Moon is poorly placed, or during **Vishti** <note>an inauspicious astrological timing</note>, or on a day corrupted by bad astronomical yogas, [one should wait]. Once recovered, a man should bathe and then immediately perform acts of charity. 91.

### **Now, Bloodletting and the Associated Bath**

Under the star **Revati** <note>original: "Paushna"</note>, bloodletting and the subsequent bath should occur at a favorable time on an auspicious day. For the sick, the discharge of blood during a forbidden time is remembered as harmful.

### **Now, Going Outside after Recovery from Illness**

One should venture out [for the first time after sickness] on an auspicious lunar day when the Moon is favorable. For one who has recovered from illness, exiting the house during a good ascendant <note>Lagna</note> is auspicious.

### **Now, the Bath for the Holi Festival**

...when the Moon is favorable and on a beautiful, auspicious lunar day. After the **Holika** <note>the ceremonial bonfire of the Holi festival</note>, one should bathe; worship is performed on the days of Mercury, Saturn, or Tuesday. 92.

### **Now, Cleansing Rites**

Under **Jyeshtha**, the two [Phalgunis], **Bharani**, **Purva-Ashadha**, **Mula**, **Shravana**, and **Magha**; on the **Jaya** <note>3rd, 8th, 13th</note> and **Purna** <note>5th, 10th, 15th</note> lunar days; 93. when Jupiter and the Moon are strong, and under **Jyeshtha**, **Punarvasu**, or **Shatabhisha**, but avoiding Saturdays, Tuesdays, and the 14th and 8th lunar days. 94.

In the birth month, if [unclear] is in a center house <note>Kendra</note> or in the ascendant, it is auspicious. The construction of dams <note>original: "setu"; refers to bridges or embankments</note> is recommended during an auspicious Ascendant <term>Lagna: the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon</term> and on an auspicious lunar day. 100.

### **Now, the Activities for Jain Teachers and Heretics**

Under the stars **Ashwini**, **Mrigashira**, **Swati**, **Punarvasu**, and **Shravana**; and when the Ascendant is ruled by Venus, Mercury, or Saturn, the activities of the Charvaka <note>an ancient school of Indian materialism</note>, the Jains, and various heretical sects <note>original: "pakhanda"; a term used by orthodox Vedic writers to describe non-Vedic or heterodox groups</note> should be carried out. 1.

<margin>21</margin> Regarding the commencement of music and drumming: the beginning of activities involving the drum <note>original: "tavala"; likely referring to the tabla or a similar percussion instrument</note> and vocal arts should occur under **Bharani**, **Rohini**, and **Shravana**, specifically on a Saturday. 1.

### **Now, the Work of Performers and Dancers**

<margin>21</margin> Under the stars **Chitra**, **Ardra**, **Rohini**, **Pushya**, the three **Uttaras** <note>Uttara Phalguni, Uttara Ashadha, and Uttara Bhadrapada</note>, and **Shravana**; on a Sunday, the performances of actors and dancers for the royal assembly are declared favorable. 2.

### **Now, the Operation of Oil Presses**

Under **Dhanishtha**, **Shatabhisha**, **Chitra**, **Anuradha**, **Pushya**, **Jyeshtha**, **Punarvasu**, and **Revati**, on a Tuesday, the operation of the oil-extraction machine <note>original: "taila yantra"</note> is auspicious, provided one avoids the "Empty" <term>Rikta: the 4th, 9th, and 14th lunar days, usually considered inauspicious for new ventures</term> and the New Moon days. 6.

### **Now, the Activities of Potters**

Under the two **Punarvasus**, **Hasta**, **Dhanishtha**, the three **Shatabhishas**, **Anuradha**, **Shravana**, and **Jyeshtha**, on a Sunday or Monday, the work of potters is prescribed by the wise. 8.

Under **Hasta**, the six stars beginning with **Ashwini**, **Pushya**, **Revati**, the three **Shravanas**, **Punarvasu**, and the pair of **Rohinis**, the work of the master carpenter <note>original: "sutradhara"; literally "the holder of the string," referring to architects and carpenters</note> should be performed. Thus ends the section on carpenters.

### **Now, the Activities of Goldsmiths**

Under the three **Shravanas**, **Ashwini**, **Pushya**, **Mrigashira**, the four **Hastas**, **Krittika**, and **Punarvasu**, during an auspicious Ascendant, the work of the goldsmith <note>original: "hemakara"</note> is favorable, except on Wednesdays and Saturdays. 11.

### **Now, the Activities of Ironworkers**

Under **Swati**, <insert><unclear>the first part of</unclear></insert> the two **Jyeshthas**, **Mula**, **Chitra**, and the three **Bharanis**, the work of those laboring with iron <note>original: "loha-shramana"</note> is especially suited to days ruled by malefic planets <note>original: "pape vare"; referring to Tuesday or Saturday</note>. 22.

### **Now, the Activities of Barbers**

Under **Jyeshtha**, the three **Hastas**, the three **Ashwinis**, **Mrigashira**, and the two **Punarvasus**, avoiding the Empty lunar days as well as the 6th and 8th days of the lunar cycle; 13. on the proper day, all fierce and standard actions of the barbers should be performed.

### **Now, the Activities of Cowherds**

Under **Vishakha**, **Punarvasu**, **Jyeshtha**, **Hasta**, **Ashwini**, and **Mrigashira**, the work of the cowherd community <note>original: "abhira jana"</note> is favored. 2. For those working with light or fire, **Shravana**, **Pushya**, and **Krittika** are suitable. 25.

### **Now, the Activities of Thieves**

Under **Vishakha**, **Krittika**, **Hasta**, **Mula**, **Ardra**, <margin>21</margin> **Bharani**, and **Magha**, and specifically when the moon is in the six signs from **Ashwini**, those who practice theft do so on Saturdays and Tuesdays. 26. Success for a thief is furthered when Mars is in the Ascendant or the tenth house.

Now, concerning the knowledge of the strength and weakness of the lunar days and the copper-plate timings for the twenty various yogas <term>Yoga: specific astronomical associations of the Sun and Moon</term>.

# Now, the Section on Solar Transits

<note>Sankranti refers to the moment the Sun moves from one zodiac sign (Rashi) to the next.</note>

If the Sun's transit occurs on a Sunday <note>original: "Bhanu-vare"</note> while the moon is in the constellation of **Bharani**, it is known as **Ghorakhya** <note>the 'Terrible'</note>. If it occurs in any of the three 'fixed' constellations, it is said to bring happiness to kings. 1.

On a Monday, if the transit occurs in the gentle constellations of **Pushya**, **Ashwini**, or **Hasta**, it is called **Dhatri**. This transit is declared to provide happiness to the merchant class <term>Vaishyas: the social class of traders and farmers</term>. 2.

When the transit happens on a Tuesday <note>original: "Kuja-ahani"</note> in the constellations of **Swati** or **Punarvasu**, it is called **Mahodari**. This particular transit brings success to thieves. 3.

On a Wednesday, if it occurs in **Ashwini**, **Revati**, **Ashlesha**, **Chitra**, or **Anuradha**, it is named **Mandakini**. This increases the prosperity of kings. 4.

When the transit occurs on a Thursday in **Rohini** or the three **Uttara** constellations <note>Uttara Phalguni, Uttara Ashadha, or Uttara Bhadrapada</note>, it is known as **Manda** and is beneficial to the priestly class <term>Brahmins: the social class of scholars and priests</term>. 5.

On a Friday, if it occurs in **Vishakha** or **Kruttika**, it is famous as **Mishra** <note>the 'Mixed'</note> and gives joy to cattle. 6.

On a Saturday, if it occurs in **Mula**, **Ardra**, **Ashlesha**, or **Jyeshtha**, it is called **Rakshasi**. It brings happiness to the lowest social classes <term>Antyaja: those performing labor outside the traditional four-fold caste system</term>. 7.

The transit's timing also affects society: if it occurs in the first portion <note>original: "ansha"</note> of the day, it harms the king; in the second, it harms the priests; in the third, it harms the merchants; and in the fourth, it harms the laborers <note>Shudras</term>. 8.

Regarding night transits: in each watch of the night, it sequentially harms the easterners, demons, actors, and herdsmen. If the transit occurs at dawn, it harms those of all religious orders. 9.

When the Sun enters **Scorpio**, **Taurus**, **Leo**, or **Aquarius**, it is called **Vishnupadi**. When it enters **Pisces**, **Virgo**, **Gemini**, or **Sagittarius**, it is called **Shadashitimukha**. 10.

The entry into **Cancer** is called the Southern Transit <term>Dakshinayana: the sun's southward journey</term> and **Capricorn** is the Northern Transit <term>Uttarayana: the sun's northward journey</term>. The entry into **Aries** or **Libra** is known as the **Equinox** <note>original: "Vishuva"</note>. 11.

The sixteen hours <term>Nadis: a unit of time equal to 24 minutes</term> before and after the transit are considered holy. For **Cancer**, the merit is in the thirty hours before; for **Capricorn**, it is the forty hours after. 12.

If the transit occurs at midnight, the merit is divided between the previous and the next day. If it occurs before midnight, the merit is on the previous day; if after midnight, the merit is on the following day until noon. 13.

If the transit occurs after sunset but before the dead of night, the merit is observed earlier. For the Sun's entry into **Capricorn** at night, the holy time for rituals is the following day before noon. 14.

If the Sun enters **Cancer** before sunrise, the entire preceding day is holy. If it enters after sunrise, the following day is holy. 15, 16.

If the transit into **Capricorn** occurs while the sun is half-set, the merit belongs to the next day. 17.

If the **Cancer** transit occurs three hours before sunrise, the merit is throughout the entire preceding day; otherwise, it is the next day. 18.

In the **Vishnupadi** transits (the fixed signs), the merit is in the time leading up to the transit. In the **Equinoxes**, the merit is centered. In the **Shadashitimukha** and the **Northern/Southern solstices**, the merit follows the transit. This applies even when calculating the transit of the "Tropical" Sun <note>original: "Sayana-ravi"; referring to the sun's position adjusted for the precession of the equinoxes</note>.

<summary>This page details the classification of solar transits (Sankranti) based on weekdays and constellations, predicting their effects on different social classes. It also provides precise rules for calculating 'Punya-kala,' the spiritually meritorious window for performing rituals during these astronomical shifts.</summary>
<keywords>Sankranti, Solar Transit, Nakshatra, Punya-kala, Uttarayana, Dakshinayana, Vishnupadi, Shadashitimukha, Vedic Astrology, Muhurta Ganapati</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the technical breakdown of the Sun’s transit (Sankranti). While the previous page focused on naming the transits based on the day of the week, this page details the specific duration of the merit-earning period (Punya-kala), numerical strengths (Vimshopakas), and the personification of the transit through its physical attributes—such as its "vehicle," weapons, clothing, and food—which are used to predict social and economic shifts.</meta>

If the transit occurs then, it shall be a secret merit, greater than this, for those who are wise. In the constellations of **Punarvasu**, **Vishakha**, **Rohini**, and the three **Uttaras**, if the transit occurs, there are nineteen *muhurtas* <note>A Muhurta is a traditional unit of time, approximately 48 minutes. This refers to the auspicious window of time for rituals</note> of merit. 19.

In **Bharani**, **Ardra**, **Shatabhisha**, **Swati**, and **Jyeshtha**—the "lower" constellations—there are twenty *muhurtas* measured during that transit. 20. For the constellations declared as "even," the transit is said to be thirty *muhurtas* long; the wise know the fruit of this at the rising of the moon. 21.

### The Vimshopaka Strengths
<note>Vimshopaka is a traditional scoring system used in Indian astrology to calculate the "strength" or "weight" of a planetary event, usually based on a scale of 20.</note>

<margin>22</margin> In the transit of Cancer <note>original: "Karka Sankrame"</note>, the scores are as follows: For the Moon, twenty; for Mars, eight; for Mercury, five; and for Saturn, five. 22. Eighteen are measured for Venus, and the same number for Jupiter on its day. For the Sun, it is declared to be favorable... <unclear>vavatushyade</unclear>. 23. However, if Rahu <note>the lunar node associated with eclipses</note> is present during the transit, it is not auspicious. In the five-fold divisions starting with Gara, the middle and lower halves of the year are affected. 24.

The "Vehicle" or mount of the transit arrives in this order: Lion, Tiger, Boar, Donkey, Elephant, Buffalo, Horse, Dog, Goat, Cow, and Rooster. 25.

The colors of the transit's garments are said to be: White, yellow, green, red, black, dark-blue, variegated <note>original: "Chitram"</note>, blanket-like, and cloud-like, in that order. 26.

The weapons held by the personified transit are, in order: A *bhushundi* <note>a spiked club or early projectile weapon</note>, a mace, a sword, a staff, a bow, an iron-tipped spear, a lance, a noose, a goad, a trident, and an arrow. 27.

The foods associated with the transit are: Plain grain, milk-pudding <note>original: "Payasam"</note>, snacks, cooked food, milk, yogurt, variegated rice, molasses, wine, ghee, sugar, and boiled rice. 28.

The ointments <note>original: "vilepanam"</note> for the transit are known as: Musk, saffron, sandalwood, yellow pigment, lac, orpiment, turmeric, collyrium, eaglewood <note>original: "aguru"</note>, and camphor. 30.

### The Classifications of the Transit
The types of beings associated with the transit are: Divine, earthly, serpents, birds, and the classes of priests, warriors, merchants, and laborers, in that order <term>The four varnas: Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra</term>.

The flowers or plants are: Punnaga, Bakula, Ketaki, Bilva, Karaka, Durva grass, Tamarind, Patala, and Vajra. 

<term>The Ornaments</term>
Anklets, tiny bells, pearls, coral, and bracelets. 32. Further, the *gunja* berry, <margin>22</margin> cowrie shells, sapphires, diamonds, and gold.

### The Ages of the Transit
<note>The Sun's transit is personified as a woman whose age determines the outcome.</note>
A small child, a young maiden, a widow, a middle-aged woman, a mature woman, a bold woman, 33, an old woman, a barren woman, a very old woman, and a yogini <note>a female ascetic</note>.

### The Results of Vehicles and Other Factors
Regarding the vehicles, castes, weapons, and foods mentioned: those who live by or trade in these specific things 34 shall face destruction, and items like clothing shall become very expensive <note>original: "maharghata"</note>. The same destruction is known to occur based on whether the transit personification is standing, sitting, or sleeping. 35.

From the birth star, calculate five parts. On the day of the transit, the effect is determined by the group of seven constellations. Starting from the path of the transit, in the first six constellations there is happiness; in the next three, there is suffering. 

Following those, in the next eight constellations, happiness is to be known; then in the next three, there is a loss of wealth. Through these auspicious signs, one achieves the acquisition of wealth; thus, the results are recorded month by month in order. If the transit occurs on a day of the week that is favorable to one's birth star, it shall be auspicious. If it falls on a "cruel" or malefic day <note>original: "papavare" — referring to days ruled by Mars, Saturn, or the waning Moon</note>, the wise know that the results for that month will be unfavorable. 1? 

### Charity and Rituals for the Transit
Now, regarding charity during the six transits. To remedy the faults of a transit...

<margin>For the sake of peace, draw a triangle or a triple-loop <unclear>bh</unclear> symbol. On a diagram of sesame seeds, one should draw the circle of 42. One should give this along with a small jewel. Now, regarding the intercalary month <term>Adhimasa: an extra month added to the Hindu lunar calendar every few years to keep it synchronized with the solar seasons</term> and the lost month. If a lunar month passes without a Sun’s transit, that is called an *Adhimasa*. This should be known as a period of higher purification in the seven rituals starting with the ancestral rites. 43. A year without a transit is a "level" year. Sometimes this occurs in the month of Kartika. This is called the "Twin" month.</margin>

<margin>It is to be known.</margin>

There may be two intercalary months in a year. 44. These are known as "soiled months" <note>original: "Malamasa"</note> and are considered forbidden for all major sacred ceremonies. 

### The Strength of the Planets
Now, regarding the strength derived from the stars and other factors. The strength of the stars is greater than the strength of the Moon; the strength of the Sun is greater than that of the Moon. The strength of all the planets, whether auspicious or inauspicious, is to be known through the Sun. 

Thus ends [this section] by the illustrious astrologer Ravala Harishankara.
